Текст представлен в латинской транслитерации IAST. Источник: sanskrit-kosha/kosha. Оцифровка: Dr. Dhaval Patel и Shree Ramkrishna Knowledge Foundation (SRKKF). Лицензия GNU GPL v3.0. # Trikāṇḍaśeṣa

## Opening

## Opening / fragment 1

\|\| śrīḥ \|\|

atha saṭīkatrikāṇḍaśeṣakoṣasya-

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / 1 \[unit 2\]

namaḥ samyaksambuddhāya \|

jayanti santaḥ kuśalaṃ prajānāṃ namo munīndrāya surāḥ smṛtāśca \|

stutā'si vāgdevi dayasva mātarvidhehi vighnādhipa maṅgalāni \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / 2 \[unit 3\]

alaukikatvādamaraḥ svakośe na yāni nāmāni samullilekha \|

vilokya tairapyadhunā pracāramayaṃ prayatnaḥ puruṣottamasya \|\| 2 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / 3 \[unit 4\]

vargakramastathā nāmaliṅgayostūpadeśatā \|

paribhāṣādikaṃ sarvamatrāpyamarakoṣavat \|\| 3 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 5\]

svarloko devalokaḥ syādavarohaḥ phalodayaḥ \|

mandaraḥ sairibhaḥ śakrabhavanaṃ khaṃ divaṃ nabhaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 6\]

svāhābhujastu vasulāḥ pūjitāśca cirāyuṣaḥ \|

śaubhā nilimpā dyuṣadaḥ svargiṇaśca nabhaḥsadaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 7\]

ṛnāmā tvaditirdevamātā syādatha pūrbajāḥ \|

pitaraścandragolasthā nyastaśastrāḥ svadhābhujaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 8\]

kavyavālādayaste ca daityāstu praghasāḥ kharāḥ \|

kratudviṣaśceṣṭimuṣo daityamātā ditirdanuḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 9\]

buddho'kaniṣṭhagamahāmunidharmacakrā

rāgāśanistriśaraṇaḥ khasamo daśārhaḥ \|

tāpī guṇākaramahāsukhavajriṇaśca

maitrībalāsamajitārisamantabhadrāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 10\]

mahābodhirdharmadhātuḥ śvetaketurjinaḥ svajit \|

trimūrtirdaśabhūmīśaḥ pañcajñāno bahukṣamaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 10 \[unit 11\]

sambuddhaḥ karuṇākūrcaḥ sarvadarśī mahābalaḥ \|

viśvabodho dharmakāyaḥ saṃgupto'rhansuniścitaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 11 \[unit 12\]

vyāmābho dvādaśākhyaśca vītarāgaḥ subhāṣitaḥ \|

sarvārthasiddhastu mahāśramaṇaḥ kaliśāsanaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 12 \[unit 13\]

gopeśaścāthā'sya yaśodhareyo rāhulaḥ sutaḥ \|

devadatto'nujo'tha syuḥ śrāvakāḥ śiṣyasaṃjñinaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 13 \[unit 14\]

pratyekabuddhāḥ khaḍgāḥ syuḥ sudāntāścaikacāriṇaḥ \|

svayambhuvo'tha mārīcī trimukhā vajrakālikā \|\| 13 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 14 \[unit 15\]

vikaṭā vajravārāhī gaurī pautrirathā ca sā \|

lokanāthastu lokeśaḥ sarojo guṇasāgaraḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 15 \[unit 16\]

lokeśvaraḥ kṛpādvaitaḥ sudhāvarṣī halāhalaḥ \|

sukhāvatīśvaraḥ kanyārāmo'sitābhraśekharaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 16 \[unit 17\]

cintāmaṇiḥ padmapāṇirbahurūpaḥ khasarpaṇaḥ \|

anīcadarśī kāraṇḍavyūhaḥ potalakapriyaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 17 \[unit 18\]

jaṭādharo mahāvīryo vakrasūryo'valokitaḥ \|

tārā mahāśrīroṅkārā svāhāśrīśca manoramā \|\| 17 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 18 \[unit 19\]

tāriṇī ca jayā'nantā śivā lokeśvarātmajā \|

khadūravāsinī bhadrā veśyālī ca sarasvatī \|\| 18 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 19 \[unit 20\]

śaṃkhinī ca mahātārā vasudhārā dhanandadā \|

trilocanā locanā syānmaṇibhadrastu jambhalaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 20 \[unit 21\]

pūrvayakṣo jalendro'tha mañjuśrīrjñānadarpaṇaḥ \|

mañjubhadro mañjughoṣaḥ kumāro'ṣṭāracakravān \|\| 20 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 21 \[unit 22\]

sthiracakro vajradharaḥ prajñākāryaśca vādirāṭ \|

nīlotpalī mahārājo nīlaḥ śārdūlavāhanaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 22 \[unit 23\]

dhiyāṃpatiḥ pūrvajinaḥ khaḍgī daṇḍī vibhūṣaṇaḥ \|

bālavrataḥ pañcavīraḥ siṃhakeliḥ śikhādharaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 23 \[unit 24\]

vāgīśvaro'tha hevakro heruścakrasaṃvaraḥ \|

devo vajrakapālī ca niśambhaḥ śaśiśekharaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 24 \[unit 25\]

vajrakīṭo'tha maitreyo yuvarājño'jitaśca saḥ \|

cīvarī śramaṇo bhikṣurbalyaścaitro'tha celukaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 25 \[unit 26\]

śrāmaṇeraḥ pravrajito mahāpāsakagominau \|

atha haṃsaratho brahmā viriñco'ṣṭaśravaḥ sanat \|\| 25 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 26 \[unit 27\]

kañjaḥ prāṇaśca vedīśavedagarbhau purāṇagīḥ \|

abjajo viśvaretāśca kamalaḥ prapitāmahaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 27 \[unit 28\]

nābhijanmā śatadhṛtirbāhmī tu brahmakanyakā \|

vāgdevī śāradā śuklā mahādhatā sarasvatī \|\| 27 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 28 \[unit 29\]

atha haritabinduśrīkaraśrīvarāhā-

jitaparapuruṣaśrīgarbhaṣaḍbindvanantāḥ \|

narakajidṛtadhāmā keśavo janmakīlaḥ ṛtupuruṣanṛsiṃhaśranthajahnūccadevāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 29 \[unit 30\]

kokaḥ purāṇapuruṣo nalineśayaśca

vāsunernarāyaṇapunarvasuviśvarūpāḥ \|

aḥ śrīnivāsadharaṇīdharavāmanaika-

śṛṅgāśca vṛṣṇivṛṣaśatrudaśāvatārāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 30 \[unit 31\]

somagarbhādidevādivarāhasvarṇabindavaḥ \|

gadāgrajo muñjakeśī sadāyogī sanātanaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 31 \[unit 32\]

rantidevaśivakīrtanau tripātsomasindhurapi rāhumūrdhachit \|

kālanemiyavanāripāṇḍavābhīlasindhuvṛṣakṛṣṇakeśinaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 32 \[unit 33\]

hemaśaṃkhaḥ śatāvartī manusātvatavāriśāḥ \|

varddhamānaḥ satānando jagannāthaḥ suyāmunaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 33 \[unit 34\]

pūtanādhenukāriṣṭakeśicāṇūrasūdanaḥ \|

bhūkaśyapastvasya pitā dundukṣityaditiḥ prasūḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 34 \[unit 35\]

syandanastu śatānandaḥ sārathiścāsya dārukaḥ \|

turaṅgāḥ śaibyasugrīvameghapuṣpabalāhakāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 35 \[unit 36\]

dhvajo bhujaṅgahā mantrī pavanavyādhiruddhavaḥ \|

śaineyastu śinernaptā yuyudhānaśca sātyakiḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 36 \[unit 37\]

balabhadrastu hālaḥ syātsaunandī halatālabhṛt \|

saṃvartakī guptacaro rukmidāryyekakuṇḍalaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 37 \[unit 38\]

madhupriyo'tha pradyumno jarābhīruḥ śamātakaḥ \|

prasūnānipañceṣū kañjano madhusārathiḥ \|

rāgarajjuḥ kalākelimadhudīparavīṣavaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 38 \[unit 39\]

iḥ keturmakarāṅkaḥ saṅkalpabhavo'ṅgajaśca rūpāstraḥ \|

raṇaraṇako ratiramaṇaḥ saṃsāragururmanojanmā \|\| 38 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 39 \[unit 40\]

kārṣṇiścā'sya priyā rāgalatā kelikalā ratiḥ \|

seḥ kelatī cā'sya bāṇā mohanonmādanau tathā \|\| 39 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 40 \[unit 41\]

santāpanaḥ śoṣaṇaśca niśceṣṭīkaraṇo'pi ca \|

suto'niruddhaḥ syādviśvaketuḥ prītitṛṣau sute \|\| 40 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 41 \[unit 42\]

śrī ca lakṣmī calā sā mā ramājaladhije'ndirā \|

maṇiḥ syamantako haste viṣṇorurasi kaustubhaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 42 \[unit 43\]

tārkṣyaḥ śilaukaḥ kāmāyuraruṇo varajaścirāt \|

pakṣisiṃha ulūtīśo vajratuṇḍaḥ sudhāharaḥ \|

svarṇapakṣaḥ sumukhasūḥ śālmalī bhujagāśanaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 43 \[unit 44\]

śambhurbhagālī kapiśāñjanaśca hīraḥ kaṭaprūrbharurerihāṇau \|

purāripañcānanabhairavāṭṭahāsaprahāsāśca khakuntalaśca \|\| 43 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 44 \[unit 45\]

kaṇṭhekālajayantaguhyaguravo jotiṅgagopālakau

piṅgākṣaḥ kaṭamardarevatajaṭāṭaṅkāvyayāḥ kūṭakṛt \|

candrāpīḍamahānaṭau ca śamiro haḥ śailadhanvā jaṭā-

cīro'tharvaṇanandivardhanaguḍākeśāgrakālañjarāḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 45 \[unit 46\]

mihirāṇo jagadyoniṣṭaṅkaṭīko'bjavāhanaḥ \|

kṛtaṅkaro varo buddhaḥ supratāpo'kutaścalaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 46 \[unit 47\]

uḥ sthāṇuḥ śipiviṣṭaśca maḥ kīlo dharmavāhanaḥ \|

virūpaviṣamatryakṣāḥ sandhyānaṭī vṛṣāñjanaḥ \|\| 46 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 47 \[unit 48\]

ekapādbhautiko bhadrorvaretāḥ pāṃśucandanaḥ \|

asthimālī śivaścā'sya khaṭvāṅgatu sukhaṃghuṇaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 48 \[unit 49\]

ajagavo'jagāvaśca vṛṣo bhṛṅgī riṭistvasau \|

bhṛṅgariṭaḥ śalo bhṛṅgī nāḍīdeho'sthivigrahaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 49 \[unit 50\]

mahākālī mahābhīmo mahākāyo vṛṣāṇakaḥ \|

dvāḥsthastu nandīśālaṅkāyanastāṇḍavatālikāḥ \|\| 49 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 50 \[unit 51\]

syānnandikeśvaro'thā'rthā hiṇḍī śikharavāsinī \|

śaktiḥ siṃharathā golā sinīvālī dṛṣadvatī \|\| 50 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 51 \[unit 52\]

sauḥ kṛṣṇapiṅgalā lambā śaileyī gaṇanāyikā \|

vārālikaikānaṃśā ca śivadūtī yamasvasā \|\| 51 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 52 \[unit 53\]

koṭavī bābhravī kaiṭabhī kaiṭabhā yādavī karburī ceśvarī ceśvarā \|

bhrāmarī dakṣakanyā ca barhidhvajī nandaputrī ca mārī ca kālañjarī \|\| 52 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 53 \[unit 54\]

gautamī tāmasī ṣaṣṭhī jayantī śūladhṛk satī \|

siṃhastasyā manastālaḥ sakhyau ca vijayājaye \|\| 53 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 54 \[unit 55\]

vajraśuṇḍaḥ karimukhaḥ pṛṣṇiśṛṅgo gaṇāgraṇīḥ \|

tridhāturekadantaśca dvideho mūṣikāñcanaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 55 \[unit 56\]

vighnahārī kumārastu svāmī dvādaśalocanaḥ \|

bālacaryaḥ siddhasenaḥ kṛkavākudhvajaśca saḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 56 \[unit 57\]

devarājarastu laḥ kauśiko'sanmahā-

bāhudanteyadalmīvāṇo'dribhit \|

vajrapāṇirmahendraḥ sugrāmaṇī-

rgrāmanemirvṛṣā nākanātho hariḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 57 \[unit 58\]

prācīnabarhiḥ khadiraḥ kapilāśvo varakratuḥ \|

prācīpatistapastaṅkaḥ pulomāriśca māhiraḥ \|\| 57 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 58 \[unit 59\]

pṛtanāṣāḍ priyātvasya cārudhārā śatāvarī \|

śacī sutī devasenā mātalistu hayaṅkaṣaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 59 \[unit 60\]

uccaiḥśravāḥ śvetahayo vṛṣaṇāśvo'marāvatī \|

vṛṣabhāsā surapurī vanamaindraṃ vṛṣaṇvasu \|\| 59 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 60 \[unit 61\]

nandanaṃ kandasāraṃ syānmiśrakāvanamityapi \|

hastimallaścaturdanto bhadrareṇurmadāṃvaraḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 61 \[unit 62\]

śvetadvipaḥ sudāmā'tha vajro'strī girikaṇṭakaḥ \|

vajrāśanirbhidurbhidro jambhāristridaśāyudham \|\| 61 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 62 \[unit 63\]

śatradhāraṃ śatāraṃ cābhrotthaṃ bhidiramambujam \|

mārjārakarṇī cāmuṇḍā karṇamoṭiśca carcikā \|\| 62 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 63 \[unit 64\]

vidyādharaḥ kāmarūpī khecaraḥ sthirayauvanaḥ \|

sparśānandā ratimadā'psarasaḥ sumadātmajā \|\| 63 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 64 \[unit 65\]

devodyānāni vaibhrājaṃ miśrakaṃ sidhrakāvaṇam \|

caitrarathaṃ ca nāsikyau yujau dasrau gadāgadau \|\| 64 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 65 \[unit 66\]

śuṣmaghāsidhasavo'ñcatirjuhūrbāṇa īṣiratuṣagrahāśirāḥ \|

chāgavāhanapṛdākubarhiṣaḥ svarṇasaptaghṛtamantradīdhitiḥ \|\| 65 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 66 \[unit 67\]

parparīkahavanātmanipīthāḥ saptajihvasamidhāvasitārciḥ \|

nāciketabhujibhārataviśvapsāḥ śuciḥ pacisamantabhujau ca \|\| 66 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 67 \[unit 68\]

vaḍavāgnistu salilendhanaḥ syādvaḍavāmukham \|

kākadhvajo vāṇijaśca skandhāgniḥ sthūlakāṣṭhadhṛk \|\| 67 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 68 \[unit 69\]

chāgaṇastu karīṣāgnistuṇāgniḥ syāttaratsamaḥ \|

tuṣānalaḥ kukūlaḥ syānmurmuro'thāgnikukkuṭaḥ \|\| 68 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 69 \[unit 70\]

saṅkilo dahanolkā syādaṅgāraḥ kokilājjhulauṃ \|

dhūmo'mbhasūrmarudvāhakhatamālaśikhidhvajāḥ \|\| 69 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 70 \[unit 71\]

agnivāhastarīḥ svāhā tva'gnāyī dahanapriyā \|

jīviteśo yamaḥ śīrṇapādaśca mahiṣadhvajaḥ \|\| 70 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 71 \[unit 72\]

mando'sya kāntā dhūmorṇā citraguptastu lekhakaḥ \|

bhṛtyau caṇḍamahācaṇḍau kālīmī tu vicārabhūḥ \|\| 71 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 72 \[unit 73\]

pañjikā tvagrasandhānī nṛcakṣāstu khasātmajaḥ \|

kīlālapaḥ palāśī ca kṣapāṭo naraviśvaṇaḥ \|\| 72 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 73 \[unit 74\]

sandhyābalo rātrimaṭho hanūṣaḥ śamanīṣadaḥ \|

palāśo vikhuraḥ śaṃkuḥ kāpīśeyastu piṇḍakaḥ \|\| 73 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 74 \[unit 75\]

piśāco meghanādastu varuṇo vaḥ parañjayaḥ \|

daityadevaśca vātistu nabhaḥprāṇo jagadbalaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 75 \[unit 76\]

bhogikāntaḥ kampalakṣmāyaśca dhārāvaliśca vāḥ \|

khaśvāsaḥ kṣipaṇurdhūlidhvajaḥ sarayuraṅkatiḥ \|\| 75 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 76 \[unit 77\]

vātulo vātagulmaḥ syāccaravāyurnidāghajaḥ \|

jhañjhānilaḥ prāvṛṣijo vāsanto malayānilaḥ \|\| 76 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 77 \[unit 78\]

śrīdadhanakelimayurāṭśvetodararatnagarbhanidhināthāḥ \|

kutanukuhecchāvasavaḥ piśācakī satyasaṅgastriśirāḥ \|\| 77 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 78 \[unit 79\]

astrī padmo mahāpadmaḥ śaṃkho makarakacchapau \|

mukundakundanīlāśca kharvāḥ syurnidhayo nava \|\| 78 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 79 \[unit 80\]

suto'sya māyurājaḥ syāttathā varṇakavirnidhau \|

āsaṅginī ca vātālī syādvātyā vātamaṇḍalī \|\| 79 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / svargavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 81\]

iti svargavargaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vyomavargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vyomavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 82\]

kunābhiḥ syādvihāyastu khaṃ bharunmeghavartma ca \|

akṣaraṃ cātha

iti vyomavargaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 1 \[unit 83\]

pāthodaḥ khatamālo gadāmbaraḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 2 \[unit 84\]

madayitnurvāyudārurvyomadhūmo nabhogajaḥ \|

gaḍeraḥ kandharo vātaratho vārimasiḥ striyām \|\| 2 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 3 \[unit 85\]

karakā syāddhanakapho bījodakamirācaram \|

meghāsthivarṣaṇantu syādrodhṛtañca parāmṛtam \|

saudāmnī tvaśanividyudaṇubhā vā'ciradyutiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 4 \[unit 86\]

tithipraṇīrdarśavipanniśāmaṇistrinetracūḍāmaṇitrinetrabhūḥ \|

sudhāṅgamau dvau mṛgapiṣlupakṣajau daśāśvasipragrahamemicandirāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 5 \[unit 87\]

chāyāmṛgadharo rājā māḥ parijño balakṣaguḥ \|

dvijo dākṣāyaṇī tuṅgī rohiṇī yajvanāmpatiḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 6 \[unit 88\]

uḍupaḥ parvadhiḥ kledo jayantastapaso hariḥ \|

tārāpīḍaḥ khacamaso vikasaḥ sindhunandanaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 7 \[unit 89\]

candrikā candraśālā syānnīhāraḥ khaniśājalam \|

śītaṃ tu śītalaṃ patrahimaṃ tu himadurdinam \|\| 7 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 8 \[unit 90\]

kujjhuṭirdhūmamahiṣī ratāṃghrī ca kuhelikā \|

dhūmikā ca nabhoreṇurathā'gastyāgnimārutī \|\| 8 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 9 \[unit 91\]

satyāgnirvāruṇirvindhyakūṭo vātāpisūdanaḥ \|

dīptāgnirvaśeyaśca samudracūluko'sya tu \|\| 9 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 10 \[unit 92\]

priyī kauṣītakī lopāmudrākhyā ca vidarbhajā \|

prākphālgunethastū'tathyānujaḥ pāruṣyagorathau \|\| 10 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 11 \[unit 93\]

dīdivirdvādaśakaraścakṣāḥ suragururguruḥ \|

śukro bhaḥ śataparveśo maghābhūrbhṛgurāsphujit \|\| 11 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 12 \[unit 94\]

ṣoḍaśārcirnavārcistu maṅgalaḥ kholmukaḥ kujaḥ \|

budho hemā rājaputra ekadehaḥ praharṣaṇaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 13 \[unit 95\]

syātpañcārciḥ śraviṣṭhājaḥ śyāmāṅgaikāṅgarodhanāḥ \|

śanaiścaro nīlavāsā mandaśchāyātmajaḥ śaniḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 14 \[unit 96\]

rāhustu grahakallolo'bhrapiśāco'pyathā'hikaḥ \|

keturjyotirathastu syādgrahādhāro dhruvaśca saḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 15 \[unit 97\]

tatkṣaṇaṃ sūryyamuktādigbudhairaṅgāriṇī smṛtā \|

āsannāstamayā dhīrairucyate sopadhūpitā \|\| 15 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 16 \[unit 98\]

sunītirdhruvamātā syānmātā dakṣasya māriṣā \|

chāyāpatho devapathaḥ somadhārā nabhaḥsarit \|\| 16 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 17 \[unit 99\]

dinapraṇīstva'vyadhiṣau bhānemiravaravrataḥ \|

gabhastihastoṃ'śudharaḥ kharāṃśurbhāravirbhagaḥ \|

vedodayaḥ pratidivā mātuḥ pitustamopahaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 18 \[unit 100\]

dinamaṇiharihelibradhnabhākoṣabhābhṛ-

darathikhagapataṅgenādrayaḥ saptasaptiḥ \|

mihiramahirapīthāḥ kālakṛtpadmapāṇiḥ

khamaṇiraruṇaśūrāvaṃśumānaṃśumālī \|\| 18 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 19 \[unit 101\]

chāyā syāttapatī mandajananī bhūmayī varī \|

saṃjñā tu yamakālindīrevantamanudasrasūḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 20 \[unit 102\]

trasareṇurmahāvīryā svātiḥ sūryā suvarcalā \|

sureṇurdyumayī tvāṣṭrī priye cete vivasvataḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / digvargaḥ / 3 \[unit 103\]

aśmano ramaṇo'nūrurjaṭāyustvaruṇātmajaḥ \|

iti digvargaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 21 \[unit 104\]

kālastva'paṣṭurjahako bhavanto bhasadityapi \|\| 21 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 22 \[unit 105\]

pīthaḥ pīyustithaścā'tha vāsro vyuṣṭāṃśake dinam \|

uṣā prabhātaṃ gosargaḥ sāyo'tsūrau vikālake \|\| 22 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 23 \[unit 106\]

rātrī rakṣojananī niṣatvarī cakrabhedanī ghorā \|

śyāmā grāmyā doṣā tuṅgī bhautī śatākṣī ca \|\| 23 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 24 \[unit 107\]

vāsuroṣā niśākhyā ca vāsateyī tamasvinī \|

yasyāṃ mattā niśi śvānaḥ śvaniśaṃ śvaniśā ca sā \|\| 24 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 25 \[unit 108\]

niḥsampāto'rdharātraḥ syātsarvāvasara ityapi \|

uccandrāpararātrau dvau daśau'mā'māvasī ca sā \|\| 25 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 26 \[unit 109\]

bhūtā kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ paurṇamyāṃ dharmavāsaraḥ \|

śakrotsavo dhvajotthānaṃ śāradī dyūtapūrṇimā \|\| 26 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 27 \[unit 110\]

kojāgaraḥ śaratparva kaumudīcāramastriyām \|

yakṣarātristu dīpālī caitrāvalyāṃ madhūtsavaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 28 \[unit 111\]

suvasantaḥ kāmamaho vāsantī kardamī striyau \|

śrāmastu māso varṣāṃśo vatsarastu yugāṃśakaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 29 \[unit 112\]

ṛtuvṛttirmāsamālaḥ syātkālagranthirurvaṭaḥ \|

kālaprabhātaṃ śaradā prāvṛṣā tu jalārṇavaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 30 \[unit 113\]

kaumudaḥ kārtiko māsaḥ phālgunālastu phālgunaḥ \|

jyeṣṭhāmūlīyamicchanti māsamāṣāḍhapūrvajam \|\| 30 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 31 \[unit 114\]

kṛtaṃ satyayugaṃ tretā'gnāyī dvāparayajñiyau \|

kalirjharjharakaḥ karmayugaṃ pāpaṃ tu pātakam \|\| 31 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 32 \[unit 115\]

śalyaṃ tūstaṃ ca kalko'tha bhadraṃ bhallaṃ śivaṃ tathā \|

śaṃ śubhaṃ cāvyaye prāṇī sattvo rūpaṃ catuṣpadaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 33 \[unit 116\]

athātmā karmabhugdehī jīvaḥ puruṣapudgalau \|

antaryāmīśvaraścāntaḥkaraṇaṃ tu mano nigu \|\| 33 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kālavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 117\]

iti kālavargaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 1 \[unit 118\]

buddhiḥ paṇḍā vidā cārvī vicārastarkaniścayau \|

guñjā carcā'tha siddhāntottarapakṣasamādhayaḥ \|

kṛtāntaḥ pūrvapakṣastu codyaṃ gīrbhaṇitirgirā \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 2 \[unit 119\]

vācā chandastu nigamaḥ sāmnyukthaṃ svarapattanam \|

brahmākṣaraṃ syādoṃkāraḥ praśnadūtī prahelikā \|\| 2 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 3 \[unit 120\]

saṃjñā'dhivacanaṃ sadyaḥkṛtaṃ lakṣaṇanāmanī \|

vyapadeśaḥ prasiddhastu ṭaṅkāro'tha navaḥ stavaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 5 \[unit 121\]

iti dhīvargaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 1 \[unit 122\]

śabdābhilāpau tvabhidhā'bhidhānaṃ vācako dhvaniḥ \|

hrāsaḥ kuharitaścā'tha manmano gadgadadhvanau \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 2 \[unit 123\]

tūraṃ tu tūryaṃ syāttāḍayamānāstu paṭahādayaḥ \|

karvīdaḥ karatālī syāddhvaninālā tu kāhalā \|\| 2 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 3 \[unit 124\]

maḍḍuko mardalo maṃtrī ḍhakkā vijayamardalaḥ \|

dragaḍaḥ pratipattūryaṃ ḍamaruḥ sūtrakoṇakaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 4 \[unit 125\]

nālī ghaṭī yāmanālī daṇḍaḍhakkā yamerukā \|

yāmaghoṣo'tha tāmrī syānmānarandhrā vikālikā \|\| 4 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 5 \[unit 126\]

bhavarutpretapaṭaho mṛtyudaṇḍakaraśca saḥ \|

raṇatūryaṃ tu saṃgrāmapaṭaho bhayaḍiṇḍimaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 6 \[unit 127\]

bhuktatūryaṃ nṛpābhīlaṃ mallatūryaṃ mahāsvanaḥ \|

mukhavādyaṃ vakratālaṃ veṇau vivaranālikā \|\| 6 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 7 \[unit 128\]

vāravāṇiḥ pragātāsyātkathakaikanaṭau samau \|\| 7 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / dhīvargaḥ / 6 \[unit 129\]

iti śabdādivargaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 130\]

sarvaveśī layārambhaḥ syāttālāvacaro naṭaḥ \|

layaputrī naṭī nartūḥ khaḍgadhārādinarttake \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 131\]

plavakaḥ kelakaścā'tha bhaṇḍaścāṭupaṭuḥ samau \|

gāyano gāthakaḥ strīṇāṃ hallīṣaṃ sahanartanam \|\| 2 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 132\]

vātsalyaśāntau tu rasau śṛṅgāraḥ kauśikaḥ smṛtaḥ \|

sambhogo viprayogaśca tadbhedau varṇikā masī \|\| 3 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 133\]

masirnā dhyāmalaṃ tu syāddhayāmo'vajñā tu pāṃsanam \|

ḍamarustu camatkāraḥ prauḍhistu kiyadetikā \|\| 4 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 134\]

svedaḥ praśravaṇaṃ sipro dvayorvrīḍo naṭārthikā \|

bhāmaḥ krodhe niḥsamastu śoke vīryaṃ śuṭīratā \|\| 5 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 135\]

chale miṣaṃ ca vaidagdhī bhaṅgiścebhanimīlikā \|

utkaṇṭhā ruharuhikā havyāsastvaticintiyā \|\| 6 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 136\]

syāccintā cintayā'tho'dvāhanaṃ raṇaraṇaṃ ca tat \|

sotkaṇṭhā smṛtirādhyānaṃ hevāko'dhyavasāyitā \|\| 7 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 137\]

parihāsaḥ kelimukhaḥ kelirdevananarmaṇī \|

āṭopāropaṭaṅkārā bhavedārabhaṭī striyām \|\| 8 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 138\]

romodgamastu pulakastvapuṣpaṃ ca tvagaṃkuraḥ \|

svadoṣagūhanaṃ bhrakṣo rāgasaṃgau tu gṛdhratā \|\| 9 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 10 \[unit 139\]

kāmacāraḥ sa yaccite pāpamādhāya śaṃsanam \|

kaukṛtyaṃ vipratīsāro vibādhā tu viheṭhanam \|\| 10 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 11 \[unit 140\]

duḥkhaloko bhavaścaiva saṃsāraḥ kaṣṭacārakaḥ \|

nirvāṇamantako mokṣaḥ śītibhāvaḥ śamo'mṛtam \|\| 11 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / nāṭyavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 141\]

iti nāṭyavargaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 142\]

adho'vyayaṃ syātpātāle saptadhā tadudīritam \|

atalaṃ sutalaṃ caiva vitalaṃ ca gabhastimat \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 143\]

talaṃ pratalapātāle virokaṃ śvabhramastriyām \|

tamasaṃ tu niśācarma nīlapaṃkaṃ rajovalam \|\| 2 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 144\]

dikkaṇṭako viyadbhūtiḥ khatvagvṛtro'tha nāgabhit \|

dṛmbhūḥ strī syādvalūtājagarāvatha halāhalaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 145\]

brahmasarpo'śvalālā ca dvimukhahirahīraṇiḥ \|

rājāhiralagardastu kālasarpo mahāviṣaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 146\]

māṇḍapuṣpastu bhujago bhavetkaukkuṭikandalaḥ \|

gonāsagonasau hālāhalaṃ hālahalaṃ viṣam \|\| 5 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 147\]

athābbhakṣo dvirasanaḥ samakolaśca bhekabhuk \|

kumbhīnaso maṇḍalī va garalaṃ jaṃgamaṃ viṣam \|\| 6 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 148\]

ananto vāsukiḥ padmo mahāpadmo'pi takṣakaḥ \|

karkoṭiḥ kulikaḥ śaṃkha ityaṣṭau nāganāyakāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 149\]

tadbandhavastu kumudakambalāśvatarādayaḥ \|

purī bhogavatī caiṣāṃ bhoginyo nāgakanyakāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / pātālavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 150\]

iti pātālabhogivargaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / narakavargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / narakavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 151\]

akaṃ duḥkhamaghaṃ caindrī tvalakṣmīḥ kālakarṇikā \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / narakavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 152\]

iti narakavargaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 1 \[unit 153\]

pāthodhisindhumakarālayavārirāśi- gaṃgādharendujanakāstimirūrmimālī \|

vārdhirmitadrutimikoṣamahāśayāśca

klībaṃ ca śailaśibiraṃ dharaṇīplaśca \|\| 1 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 2 \[unit 154\]

pumān kacaṃga vāṃkaḥ kūlaṃkaṣaparaṃgavau \|

taranto dāradaḥ perurmahīprācīramadvayoḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 3 \[unit 155\]

nadīnaścātha kamalaṃ nīraṃ nārāḥ striyāmirā \|

kaṃ dakaṃ jalamasthānamagādhe'thāmbhasāṃbhramaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 4 \[unit 156\]

tārūro vāyugulmaśca kalalābhaḥ kalakuraḥ \|

puroṭiḥ patrajhaṅkāre daraṇiḥ kūlacuṇḍakaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 5 \[unit 157\]

culuko dhanajambāle dalāḍhye paṃkakarvaṭaḥ \|

taraṇo bhelake vāriratho naustarikaḥ plavaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 6 \[unit 158\]

hoḍastarāṃdhurvahanaḥ taraṇḍo vārvaṭaḥ pumān \|

pādārakaḥ syātpolindaḥ patrabāle tulādhaṭaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 7 \[unit 159\]

samudrakaphaḍiṇḍīrajalahasāstu pheṇake \|

kaivarte jālikaśchoṭī kupinī syādatha plavaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 8 \[unit 160\]

palavaḥ pañjarākheṭaḥ kaṇṭakī tu jaleśayaḥ \|

ātmāśī śambaro mūko'nimiṣo valkavānapi \|\| 8 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 9 \[unit 161\]

mīne vadālaḥ pāṭhīno matsyarājastu rohitaḥ \|

bhālāṃkaścātha kuliśaṃ kaṭaṃ kāṣṭīla ityapi \|\| 9 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 10 \[unit 162\]

phalakī syāccitraphalī vājagrīvo madārmadaḥ \|

kavayī krakacapṛṣṭhī kaṃkatroṭo jalavyadhaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 11 \[unit 163\]

śapharaḥ śvetakolaḥ syātkhaśeṭastu khaleśayaḥ \|

illiśe vārikarpūragāṅgeyaśapharādhipāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 12 \[unit 164\]

jalatālo'pyatha calatpūrṇimācandracañcalau \|

jalavṛścika iñcāke gaṅgāṭeyo galānilaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 13 \[unit 165\]

śālaḥ śakulagaṇḍaḥ syādandhāhiḥ kūrcikā dvayoḥ \|

brāhmī tu paṅkagaṇḍako laghugargastrikaṇṭakaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 14 \[unit 166\]

tiryagyānaḥ kulīraḥ syānmukhāstro harilocanaḥ \|

bahiḥ kuṭīcaraḥ potādhānaṃ tu syājjalāṇḍakam \|\| 14 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 15 \[unit 167\]

jalarūpastu makaro marolarasidaṃṣṭrakaḥ \|

mīnarastantunāgastu nakravāḍavahārakau \|\| 15 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 16 \[unit 168\]

nakrastu vārbhaṭaḥ syādambukiṇṭombukaṇṭakaḥ \|

jalaśūkaramāyādajhaṣāśanahradagrahāḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 17 \[unit 169\]

jalakūrmastvambukīśo vasāḍhyaḥ śiśukaśca saḥ \|

udrastu jalamārjārajalākhunakulaplāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 18 \[unit 170\]

mṛtkirā dhurghurī vārikṛmistu jalamakṣikā \|

paṭālukā jalaukāmbusarpiṇī veṇivedhinī \|\| 18 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 19 \[unit 171\]

vyaṅgastu nandako bheko gūḍhavarcāḥ kṣatālayaḥ \|

kokojihvo'tha pañcāṅgagupte kroḍāṃghrikacchapau \|\| 19 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 20 \[unit 172\]

gaḍuḥ kiñculuko'thā'mburvikiraścūḍakaśca saḥ \|

cuṇṭā cuṇṭī ca lūtā'thāndhakūpaḥ karkarāndhukaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 21 \[unit 173\]

palvalaṃ dīrghikā vāpī yaṣṭikā mīnagādhikā \|

avaghaṭṭāvaiṭau tulyau tallaṃ villaṃ talaṃ ca tat \|\| 21 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 22 \[unit 174\]

dvijaprapālavāle syātkedāraḥ pāṃśumardanaḥ \|

nadī nirjhariṇī rodhovakrā sāgaragāminī \|\| 22 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 23 \[unit 175\]

talodā cāmpilā sindhurṛṣikulyā vahā ca sā \|

avagā gāndinī gaṅgā haimavatyugraśekharā \|\| 23 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 24 \[unit 176\]

dharmadravī siddhasindhustāpī tu yamunā yamī \|

revā tu pūrvagaṃgā syānmuralā tu murandalā \|\| 24 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 25 \[unit 177\]

syādarddhagaṅgā kāverī vāśiṣṭhī gomatī same \|

atha godāvarī bhīmopalā golā ca gautamī \|\| 25 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 26 \[unit 178\]

saptagodāvaraṃ tatra kuvalaṃ tūtpalaṃ kuvam \|

kairavaṃ gandhasomaṃ syātkandoṭaśca niśāhasaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 27 \[unit 179\]

śrīkaraṃ kṛṣṇakandaṃ ca raktotpalamalipriyam \|

lakṣmīgṛhaṃ kumuccā'tha śālūkaḥ paṅkaśūraṇaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 28 \[unit 180\]

praśnī tu kumbhikā vārimūlī parṇī khamūlikā \|

syādvāricatvaro'thāmbutālaḥ salilakuntalaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 29 \[unit 181\]

śaivalo haṭhaparṇī strī kāvāraṃ vāricāmaram \|

padme kavāraṃ paṅkejaṃ bisanābhistu padminī \|\| 29 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 30 \[unit 182\]

puṭakinyevamevā'nyāḥ kundinītālinīmukhāḥ \|

viṣaṇḍaṃ tantulaviṣe śṛṅgāṭo vārikubjakaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 10 \[unit 183\]

iti vārivargaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vārivargaḥ / 1 \[unit 184\]

iti saṭīkatrikāṇḍaśeṣe prathamaḥ kāṇḍaḥ samāptaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / fragment 185

atha saṭīkatrikāṇḍaśeṣakoṣasya dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 1 \[unit 186\]

bhūrbhūtadhātrī girikarṇikābdhidvīpā samudrāmbaramekhalā kuḥ \|

kṣamā'drikīlā ca jagadvahā ca puṃsi smṛtau madhyamalokamartyau \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 2 \[unit 187\]

sahā kāntā gandhavatī ratnagarbhā ca medinī \|

svasthalī bhāratākhyaṃ tu varṣaṃ haimavataṃ viduḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 3 \[unit 188\]

bhārataṃ narabhūḥ kiṃpūruṣaṃ harivarṣaṃ tataḥ \|

bhadrāśvamuttarakururhiraṇmayamilāvṛtam \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 4 \[unit 189\]

ketumālamamūni syurbhuvo varṣāṇi vai nava \|

jambūplakṣakuśakrauñcaśākaśālmalipuṣkaraiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 5 \[unit 190\]

dvīpāḥ saptātha saptaiva samudrā api kīrtitāḥ \|

lavaṇekṣusurāsarpadadhidugdhapayomayāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 6 \[unit 191\]

ūṣaraṃ tuṣavānū brahmāvartastapovaṭaḥ \|

kurukṣetraṃ prayogaśca himādriṃ vindhyamantarā \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 7 \[unit 192\]

madhyadeśo'tha puṇḍrāḥ syurvarendrī gauḍanīvṛtiḥ \|

prabhāsaḥ somatīrthaṃ syādantarvedī kuśasthalī \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 8 \[unit 193\]

prāgjyotiṣaṃ kāmarūpe tīrabhuktistu nicchaviḥ \|

videhāścā'tha kāśmīre kīrāḥ syuḥ śāstraśilpinaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 9 \[unit 194\]

turuṣkāḥ khaśayaṣṭakkā bāhlikāśca trigartakāḥ \|

daśerakā marubhuvo mālavāḥ syuravantayaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 10 \[unit 195\]

ḍāhalāścedayaścaidyāḥ kāruṣāstu bṛhadgṛhāḥ \|

yadvastu daśārhāḥ syuḥ sātvatāḥ kukurāśca te \|\| 10 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 11 \[unit 196\]

oḍrā utkalanāmāno magadhāḥ kīkaṭā matāḥ \|

velākūlaṃ tāmaliptaṃ tāmaliptī tamālikā \|\| 11 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 12 \[unit 197\]

ekacakraṃ harigṛhaṃ śambhupuryatha vartaniḥ \|

pūrvadeśo'tha sāketamayodhyottarakośalāḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 13 \[unit 198\]

kuśasthalaṃ kānyakubjaṃ nāgāhvaṃ hastināpuram \|

gajāhvaṃ hāstinaṃ cā'tha śrāvastī dharmapattanam \|\| 13 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 14 \[unit 199\]

kurukṣetraṃ vinaśanaṃ kauśāmbī vatsapattanam \|

prājāpatyaḥ prayāgaḥ syāddvārakā vanamālinī \|\| 14 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 15 \[unit 200\]

dvāravatyabdhinagarī videhāmithile same \|

mathurā tu madhūpaghnaṃ jitvarī tu tapasthalī \|\| 15 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 16 \[unit 201\]

vārāṇasī tīrtharājī viśālojjayinī same \|

campā tu mālinī puṣpapuraṃ pāṭaliputrakam \|\| 16 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 17 \[unit 202\]

devīkoṭo bāṇapuraṃ koṭīvarṣamuṣāvanam \|

syācchoṇitapuraṃ cātha yojanaṃ mārgadhenukam \|\| 17 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 18 \[unit 203\]

krimiśailastu valmīkaḥ śakramūrdhā'tha sañcaraḥ \|

dharaṇaḥ piṇḍalaḥ setuḥ panthāstu kṣullamo vahaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 19 \[unit 204\]

vāṭaḥ pathaśca māthaśca khapurantūrdhvagaṃ puram \|

hariścandrapuraṃ śaubhamudraṅgaḥ pratimārgakaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 20 \[unit 205\]

traṅgā traṅgo nirmuṭastu paṇyājīvakacaṅgalau \|

puṃsi haṭṭakrayārohau janyaṃ grāmamukhaṃ ca tat \|\| 20 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 21 \[unit 206\]

syādabhisyandanagaraṃ śākhānagaramityapi \|

śāsanaṃ dharmakīlaḥ syānmakutiḥ śudraśāsanam \|\| 21 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 22 \[unit 207\]

paṭṭolikā klṛptakīlā pāṃśukīlaṃ na kasyacit \|

ṣaḍbhiryavaiḥ syādaṃguṣṭha etaidaśabhirbhavet \|\| 22 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 23 \[unit 208\]

vitastiḥ syādato dvābhyāṃ hastaḥ syāttaccatuṣṭayam \|

daṇḍo dhanvantaraṃ tasya sahasradvitayena tu \|\| 23 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 24 \[unit 209\]

krośastābhyāntu gavyūtistaddvayaṃ yojanaṃ matam \|

caturaṣṭaśatagrāmāntardroṇamukhakarvaṭau \|\| 24 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / bhūmivargaḥ / 1 \[unit 210\]

iti bhūmivargaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ / 1 \[unit 211\]

garbhāgārāpavarake vāstu syādgṛhapotakaḥ \|

syātprāsādo devakulaṃ putrikā śālabhañjikā \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ / 2 \[unit 212\]

oko gṛha piṭaṃ cālo vaḍamī candraśālikā \|

kūṭāgāraṃ cātha kapiśīrṣaṃ ghoṭakaśīrṣakam \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ / 3 \[unit 213\]

kramaśīrṣaṃ cātha kharakuṭī nāpitaśālikā \|

kulāyikā pakṣiśālā kārāveśma vadhāṅgakam \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ / 4 \[unit 214\]

kāyamānaṃ tṛṇakuṭī durbhaṭaṃ nibhṛtaṃ gṛham \|

kuḍaṅgodghāṭapiṭharā indrakoṣastu mañcakaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ / 5 \[unit 215\]

vāsāgāraṃ bhogagṛhaṃ kanyāpatnyāṭaniṣkuṭāḥ \|

devīgṛhaṃ tu valabhī layanaṃ saugatālayaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ / 6 \[unit 216\]

śivasya vṛṣamaṇḍapyāṃ budhairgopiṭakaṃ smṛtam \|

vātāyanaṃ gṛhākṣaṃ syādvadhūṭaśayanaṃ tathā \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ / 7 \[unit 217\]

ulapaśalyopakaṇṭhe dve kapāṭo dvārakaṇṭakaḥ \|

kavāṭaścāraraṃ kanthāvāṭaḥ prākāra ityapi \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / puravargaḥ / 2 \[unit 218\]

iti puravargaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 219\]

śailaḥ sthāvaradhātubhṛddharakukīlauvyaṅgakaḥ sānumā-

ñjīmūtaḥ pṛthuśekharaśca kaṭakī dantī nago nirjharī \|

kuṭṭāro'tha himālayo nagapatirmenādhavomāgurū

kailāso gaṇaparvataśca rajataprasthaḥ kuberācalaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 220\]

hiraṇyanābhamainākasunābhā himavatsute \|

śailāgre śikharaṃ śṛṅgaṃ dantaḥ prāgbhāramityāpi \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 221\]

pūrvādrirdinamūrdhā syādastādriścaramācalaḥ \|

candanādrista malayaḥ kauñcaḥ krauñcaśca mālyavān \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 222\]

mahendro malayaḥ sahyaḥ śuktimān gandhamādanaḥ \|

vindhyaśca pāriyātraśca saptaite ca kulācalāḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 223\]

aśmā pajharukaḥ pāṭaḥ pāraṭīṭaśca munmaruḥ \|

gharṣaṇālaḥ śilāputro gṛhāśmā gṛhakacchapaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 224\]

gairikaṃ tu vanālaktaṃ raktadhāturgaverukam \|

pratyaśmā girimṛccātha śilādhātuḥ śitopalaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 225\]

mṛtkolo varṇalekhā ca kaṭhinī kakkhaṭī khaṭī \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / śailavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 226\]

iti śailavargaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 1 \[unit 227\]

vārkṣaṃ vanaṃ talaṃ talkaṃ guhilaṃ samajaṃ trasam \|

līlodyānaṃ devanaṃ syādvīthyālī paṅktirāvalī \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 2 \[unit 228\]

vṛkṣaḥ kāraskaro gacchaḥ palāśo viṣṭaraḥ sthiraḥ \|

caityo devatarurdevāvāse karibhakuñjarau \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 3 \[unit 229\]

chāyātaruḥ sthiracchāyo vandā kākaruhā smṛtāḥ \|

vanaspatirnirluṭaḥ syātphullonnidravikasvarāḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 4 \[unit 230\]

smitonmudrau kiliñjastu sūkṣmadārvatha pallavaḥ \|

kiśalaṃ kisalaṃ cāthotkalikāṅgārite same \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 5 \[unit 231\]

guccho gulucchaḥ kṣepaḥ syāccamarī mañjaraṃ na nā \|

mañjiśca gondī gundī ca makarando marandavat \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 6 \[unit 232\]

vedhakaḥ kaṇṭake patrasūcidrunakhavaṅkilāḥ \|

pippalo vādaraṅgaḥ syāccaityadruḥ keśavālayaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 7 \[unit 233\]

yakṣo'ḍḍambarakaṃ tvasya phale'tha grahanāśanaḥ \|

devavṛkṣo dalegandhiḥ saptaparṇaḥ śirorujā \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 8 \[unit 234\]

dantakarṣaṇajambīrau katakombuprasādanaḥ \|

varuṇastva'śmarīghno'thādhvagabhogyo madhudrumaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 9 \[unit 235\]

kapicūto'mrātako'sya phale paśuharītakī \|

pikabandhustu cūtaḥ syātstrīpriyaḥ ṣaṭpadātithiḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 10 \[unit 236\]

madhudūto vasantadrurmahākālorukālakau \|

śobhāñjanastu strīcittahārī vidradhināśanaḥ \|\| 10 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 11 \[unit 237\]

prabhāñjano'tha prācīnapanaso goharītakī \|

mahākapittho bilvaśca kolirgṛdhranakhī smṛtā \|\| 11 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 12 \[unit 238\]

syātpicchaladalā svāduphalā'tho nāgaraṅgakaḥ \|

cakrādhivāsī nāraṅgaḥ kirmīrastvaksugandhakaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 13 \[unit 239\]

syādvarmaṇaḥ picchilatvagdhavastu madhuratvacaḥ \|

piśācaḥ pītaphalaḥ śākoṭaḥ karkaśacchadaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 14 \[unit 240\]

arśohitastu rakṣoghno bhallātakyatha śālmalī \|

durārohā smṛtā piṇḍītagaraḥ kaphavardhanaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 15 \[unit 241\]

kākaciñcī tulābījaṃ khadiro bālapatrakaḥ \|

yūpadruḥ kuṣṭhahṛccātha karañjo ghṛtapūrṇakaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 16 \[unit 242\]

rasāyanaphalā pathyā śakrasṛṣṭā sudhodbhavā \|

saralo dhūpavṛkṣaḥ syācchoṇako nyaṅgubhūruhaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 17 \[unit 243\]

campakoṣastu murajaphalaḥ syātpanasaśca saḥ \|

ḍahuḥ śudrāmlapanaso niculo raktamañjaraḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 18 \[unit 244\]

nimbo'rkapādapaḥ puṇyagandhastu kusumādhirāṭ \|

cayako varalabdhaśca vakulaḥ siṃhakeśaraḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 19 \[unit 245\]

sīdhugandho'tha kaṅkolirnaṭaḥ kāntāṅghridohadaḥ \|

aśokaḥ piṇḍapuṣpastu dāḍimaḥ phalaśāḍavaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 20 \[unit 246\]

dāḍimbaḥ parvaruṭ ca syātsvādvamlaḥ śukavallabhaḥ \|

piṇḍīro'tha suvarṇebhanāgākhyo nāgakeśaraḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 21 \[unit 247\]

syātpuṣparocanaścā'pi cūrṇaṃ tasya haridravaḥ \|

niśipuṣpā tu śephālī kuṭajaḥ pāṇḍuradrumaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 22 \[unit 248\]

latāśaṅkutaruḥ śālo nīpo dhārākadambakaḥ \|

kaṭaṅkaṭerī haridrā śiṃśapā yugmapatrikā \|\| 22 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 23 \[unit 249\]

paṭole syādrājaphalaṃ tasya mūle tu ramyakam \|

sūtrapuṣpastu kārpāse jīrṇaparṇaṃ kadambakam \|\| 23 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 24 \[unit 250\]

prahasantī tu yūthīsyādvamanaḥ puṣpacāmaraḥ \|

mālatīpuṣpakalikāṃ saumanasyāyanīṃ viduḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 25 \[unit 251\]

kunde syādvoraṭaḥ puṃsi graiṣmī nu navamālikā \|

raktapiṇḍastvoḍrapuṣpaḥ syādamlānaḥ kuraṇṭakaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 26 \[unit 252\]

kandalaśilīndhrapuṣpe śleṣmaghnā tripuramallikā proktā \|

eraṇḍahastikarṇau prācīnāmalakavāribadare dve \|\| 26 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 27 \[unit 253\]

bṛhatpāṭalidhustūrau kadalī tvāyatacchadā \|

syāttantuvigrahā mañjiphalā vāraṇavallabhā \|\| 27 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 28 \[unit 254\]

vātiṅgaṇastu vārtākurvārtākaḥ śākabilvakaḥ \|

klībe vaṅgaṃ ca vārttākī syānmahābṛhatītyapi \|\| 28 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 29 \[unit 255\]

kuṣṭhaṃ gadāhvayaṃ mūlaṃ tvasya puṣkaramūlakam \|

vaṅgasenastvagastidruḥ śukanāso sunidrumaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 30 \[unit 256\]

analiḥ kunalī putrañjīvastu ślīpadāpahaḥ \|

chattrako mallipatraṃ syātkuñjako vārikaṇṭakaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 31 \[unit 257\]

vāstūkaṣṭakkadeśīyaḥ śākavīro ghanāmalaḥ \|

śothajidgarahā kālaśāko'pyatha mahauṣadhī \|\| 31 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 32 \[unit 258\]

mahī viṣaghnī cakrāṅgī matsyākṣī hilamocikā \|

jalabrahmī ca śāliñce pattūro lohamārakaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 33 \[unit 259\]

pecukaṃ peculī pecurnāḍīco viśvarocanaḥ \|

keśarājo nāgamāraḥ vaparurbhṛṅgasodaraḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 34 \[unit 260\]

suniṣaṇṇaṃ cucuḥ puṃsi chatrapatraṃ tamālakam \|

sthalapadmamatho devapallī madhvālukaṃ viduḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 35 \[unit 261\]

phalapuccho varaṇḍāluḥ syādraṅgeṣṭālukaṃ tu yat \|

śrīmastakaḥ svastikaḥ syādrāhūcchiṣṭo rasonakaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 36 \[unit 262\]

kuṣmāṇḍastu ghṛṇāvāsastimiṣo grāmyakarkaṭī \|

vālukī karkaṭī vārurmūtralā romaśā ca sā \|\| 36 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 37 \[unit 263\]

syāccelānaścitraphalaḥ sukhāśo rājateniśaḥ \|

latāpanasanāṭāmlau seṭurūrdhvāsitastu yaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 38 \[unit 264\]

parāruḥ kāravello'sau goḍumbā rājacirbhaṭaḥ \|

mṛtyubījastu vaṃśaḥ syātketakaḥ krakacacchadaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 39 \[unit 265\]

dṛkkaṭo bahumūlaḥ syātkarīre veṇukarkaraḥ \|

kāśo'śvavālaḥ kākekṣurvanahāsaśca jantulā \|\| 39 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 40 \[unit 266\]

guḍadārurmadhutṛṇaṃ syādikṣurasipatrakaḥ \|

khārodako nārikelaḥ karakāmbhāḥ śiraḥphalaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 41 \[unit 267\]

surañjano gopadalo rājatālaśchaṭāphalaḥ \|

karamaṭṭastantusāro guvāko koṭa ityāpi \|\| 41 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 42 \[unit 268\]

rāmapūgastu kāmīno munipūgaḥ surevaṭaḥ \|

pūgaroṭastu hintālaḥ klībaṃ kirīṭi tatphalam \|\| 42 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 43 \[unit 269\]

ghanāmayaḥ syātkharjūro dvau tu kācimabhañjarū \|

tarau devakulodbhūte dūrvā tu haritālikā \|\| 43 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vanauṣadhivargaḥ / 4 \[unit 270\]

iti vanauṣadhivargaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 1 \[unit 271\]

siṃhaḥ kaṇṭhīravo bhīmavikrāntaḥ karimācalaḥ \|

pañcaśikhaśca śailāṭaḥ pārīndraḥ śvetapiṅgalaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 2 \[unit 272\]

marutplavaśca nādo'sya cukkāraḥ kṣveḍitaṃ ca tat \|

utpādakastu śarabhaḥ śārdūlo'ṣṭāpadaśca saḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 3 \[unit 273\]

atha hiṃsrapaśurvyāḍaḥ śvāpadaḥ śibirityapi \|

bāghnīṇasastvekacaro gaṇotsāhaśca gaṇḍakaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 4 \[unit 274\]

hiṃsāruḥ śvāpado vyāghre mahiṣastu rajasvalaḥ \|

daṃśabhīrurjarantaśca lāliko yamavāhanaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 5 \[unit 275\]

atha śūkare vanāhiradantāyudhavakravajradīrgharadāḥ \|

sūcīromā bahusūrākhanikaḥ sthūlanāsiko bhūkṣit \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 6 \[unit 276\]

eṇaḥ kuraṅgamo riśyaḥ syādṛśyaścārulocanaḥ \|

golāṅgūle kapitthāsyadadhiśoṇanagāṭanāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 7 \[unit 277\]

kikhiśca hūravastu syācchṛgālo ghorarāsanaḥ \|\|

lopāśo mṛtamattaśca vanaśvā'thaśṛgālikā \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 8 \[unit 278\]

lopāśikā dīptajihvā kikhirulkāmukhī ca sā \|

viḍālaḥ kundamo naktacārī dīptākṣajāhakau \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 9 \[unit 279\]

śaśakastu vanākhuḥ syātkhaṭṭāśī pūtiśālikā \|

gavayastu gavānūka eḍakaḥ śiśuvāhyakaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 10 \[unit 280\]

vanacchāgo'tha gandhautuḥ khaṭṭāśo vanavāsanaḥ \|

undurustuṭumo randhrababhrurdīnā tu mūṣikā \|\| 10 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 11 \[unit 281\]

syācciko veśmanakulaḥ puṃvṛṣo gandhamūṣikaḥ \|

syādvedāraḥ krakacapātkṛkalāśastṛṇāñjanaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 12 \[unit 282\]

pratisūryo'pyāñjanastu citrakolo halāhalaḥ \|

kāraskarāṭikā karṇajalaukā syādatho vaṭiḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 13 \[unit 283\]

utpādiko dehikā syādaṣṭapādastu kintanuḥ \|

prāsādakukkuṭo jhillīkaṇṭho gṛhakapotakaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 14 \[unit 284\]

pārāvato'tha śakrākhyo divāndho vakranāsikaḥ \|

harinetro divābhīto nakhāśī pīyughargharau \|\| 14 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 15 \[unit 285\]

kākabhīrurnaktacāro kaṇāṭīnastu khañjanaḥ \|

kākacchadaḥ khañjakhelastātalo muniputrakaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 16 \[unit 286\]

bhadranāmā ratanidhiḥ kāṣṭhakuṭṭaḥ śatacchade \|

dīrghapātrastu kaṅkaḥ syādgalagaṇḍastu markaṭaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 17 \[unit 287\]

cātake babhruvāpīhaghanatālatriśaṅkavaḥ \|

varṣapriyo'tha meghāvī ciriḥ kīraḥ palāśanaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 18 \[unit 288\]

kukkuṭastu vivṛtākṣo rātrivedī kalāvikaḥ \|

uṣākalo'tha tāmrākṣaḥ kākapuṣṭaḥ kuhūmukhaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 19 \[unit 289\]

madhukaṇṭho ghoṣayitnuḥ kāmatālaḥ sutārdanaḥ \|

kāko ratajvaraḥ sūtrī śrāvako gūḍhamaithunaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 20 \[unit 290\]

kāravaḥ paramṛtyuśca luṇṭāko maukulirdvikaḥ \|

śakrajaḥ satyavākkāṇo'pakṛṣṭo nagarīvakaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 21 \[unit 291\]

aindriśca nāḍījaṅgho'tha gṛdhraḥ syāddūradarśanaḥ \|

śitikaṇṭhastu dātyūhaḥ kākamadguḥ kageṭuraḥ \|

cirambhaṇastu cillaḥ syātkhabhrāntiḥ kaṇṭhanīḍakaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 22 \[unit 292\]

sūktā madanaśalākā citrākṣī śālikā'vacaṇḍā ca \|

śakunajñā tu jyeṣṭhā gṛholikāṣṭāṭṭanī mūlī \|\| 22 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 23 \[unit 293\]

dīrghajaṅgho niśaiḍaḥ syādvakoṭaḥ śuklavāyasaḥ \|

kaṅkerudvārabalibhukchikhī candravihaṅgamaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 24 \[unit 294\]

bakerukā balākā syātkuraro matsyanāśanaḥ \|

dvandvacārī cakravākaḥ sārasastu kalāṅkuraḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 25 \[unit 295\]

gonardaścitrapakṣastu tittiriḥ syātkapiñjalaḥ \|

laghumāṃso mayūrastu candrakī citramekhalaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 26 \[unit 296\]

pracalākī sitapārgaṃśikhāvalagalavratāḥ \|

mārjārakaṇṭho'tha tilamayūraḥ syātkuruṇṭakaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 27 \[unit 297\]

jhampāśano mīnaraṅko nāsācchinnā tu pūrṇikā \|

pipīlikā tu hārī syādyamakīṭastu ghurghuraḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 28 \[unit 298\]

syānmarkaṭastu śalaka āśābandho'sya jālake \|

phālakhelāgarudgodhā bhāratī vyomanāsikā \|\| 28 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 29 \[unit 299\]

cañcusūcistu sugṛhaḥ pītatuṇḍo'tha sarṣapī \|

hāputrikā khañjanikā tulikāsphoṭike same \|\| 29 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 30 \[unit 300\]

karaṭurvyādhanaḥ karcūrnaraḥ kūṇiśca vāggudaḥ \|

vartikā viṣṇuliṅgī syāllāvako laghujaṅgalaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 31 \[unit 301\]

saraḥkākastu haṃsaḥ syājjālapātpurudaṃśakaḥ \|

gobhaṇḍīraḥ paṅkakīraḥ koyaṣṭirjalakukkubhaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 32 \[unit 302\]

jīvañjīvo viṣamṛtyurjīvajīvo'tha makṣikā \|

mācikā bhambharālī ca daṃśaḥ syādbhāmbharālikaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 33 \[unit 303\]

bhāṅkārī carmacaṭyāṃ tu jatunī gṛhamācikā \|

viṣasṛkko bhṛṅgarolo varolastṛṇaṣaṭpadaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 34 \[unit 304\]

varaṭī tu varolī syādbrāhmaṇyatha tamomaṇiḥ \|

dhvāntonmeṣaśca khadyoto jyotirbījo nimeṣaruk \|\| 34 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 35 \[unit 305\]

bhramaraścañcarīkaḥ syādrolambo madhusūdanaḥ \|

indindiraḥ puṣpakoṭo madhudo madhukeśaṭaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 36 \[unit 306\]

reṇuvāso'tha maśako ghoṣo raṇaraṇaścasaḥ \|

pakṣī tu nāḍīcaraṇaḥ syāccañcabhṛdanekajaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 37 \[unit 307\]

syuḥ khagotpatavāraṅkakaṇṭhāgnivyomacāriṇaḥ \|

yugaladvandvayugmāni yugaṃ yamalayāmale \|\| 37 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / siṃhādivargaḥ / 5 \[unit 308\]

iti siṃhādivargaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 309\]

strī vadhūryoṣitā rāmā maṇḍayantī vadhūjanaḥ \|

kanyakā'lakanandā sā dūṣitā dharmakāriṇī \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 310\]

sasvedā cātha taruṇī dikkarī dhanikā ca sā \|

maruṇḍoccalalāṭā syātsaśmaśrurnaramāninī \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 311\]

snuṣā vadhūṭī madhuranisvanā haṃsagadgadā \|

sukhoṣṇaśītā tuhinagrīṣmayorvaravarṇinī \|

krodhanā bhāminī caṇḍī dārustrīśālabhañjikā \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 312\]

viraktā durbhagā saptaputrasūḥ sutavaskarā \|

pativrataikapatnī syādvidhavā jālikā matā \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 313\]

viśvastā lañjinī tu syāllakṣikā kalakūjikā \|

veśyā śūlā vāravāṇirjharjharāpyatha kuṭṭinī \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 314\]

ijyā syādratatālī ca ghaṭadāsī gaṇerukā \|

maliṣṭhā sarajāḥ ṣāṇmāturadvaimāturau samau \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 315\]

samadurduhitā putrī dvitīyaḥ kuladhārakaḥ \|

pitā janayitā vaptā śiśukaḥ kṣīrakaṇṭhakaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 316\]

bālo muṣṭindhayaścātha śyālikā kelikuñcikā \|

nāgaro devalaḥ śyālo vārakīro'tha sāśrudhīḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 317\]

śvaśrurvṛddhaḥ śatānīko jarantaḥ syādathāṃśakaḥ \|

sagandhājñātidāyādā mātulo mātukeśaṭaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 10 \[unit 318\]

bhartā bharurnarmakīlaḥ prāṇanāthaḥ sukhotsavaḥ \|

hṛdayeśo ratagururjāraḥ pāpapatiḥ samau \|\| 10 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 11 \[unit 319\]

kumārabhṛtyā syādbālatantraṃ garbhiṇyavekṣaṇam \|

garbhāṣṭamo devamāso nābhinālā'malā smṛtā \|\| 11 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 12 \[unit 320\]

ādhānikaṃ puṃsavanaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ ca tat \|

khallīṭaḥ khalitiḥ kallo badhiraḥ khorakhañjakau \|\| 12 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 13 \[unit 321\]

dorgaḍurbāhukuṇṭaḥ syādauṣadhaṃ tu bhiṣagjitam \|

tvakpuṣpikā kilāsaṃ sthācchlīpadaṃ pādagaṇḍiraḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 14 \[unit 322\]

yonyarśaḥ kandasaṃjñaḥ syātkiṇaḥ śuṣkavraṇo'pi ca \|

ajīrṇe vāyugaṇḍo'ntarvamiścapalatāśayaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 15 \[unit 323\]

pāparogo raktavaṭī masūryatha tamovraṇaḥ \|

valmīko'thātisāro'nnagandhiḥ syādudarāmayaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 16 \[unit 324\]

kuraṇḍavṛddhaḥ siṅghānaśeṭau skandanarecane \|

garbhaṇḍo nābhiguḍako'bhimantho locanāmaye \|\| 16 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 17 \[unit 325\]

yuvagaṇḍo'vagaṇḍaḥ syātpittaṃ māyuḥ palaṅkaraḥ \|

śleṣmā balāśo māṃsaṃ tu jarūthamatha śoṇitam \|\| 17 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 18 \[unit 326\]

palakṣāro'tha mastiṣko mastaluṅgo'pyatha snasā \|

vasnasā'tho'dhvaramadhu klībe vaktrāsavaḥ pumān \|\| 18 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 19 \[unit 327\]

jihvāmalaṃ tu kulukaṃ pippikā dantaje male \|

piṇḍo'striyāṃ kulaṃ kṣetraṃ śarīraṃ skandhapañjarau \|\| 19 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 20 \[unit 328\]

śastaṃ vapuḥ pudgalaṃ syādāṅgamaṅgaṃ tu komalam \|

pāyustanuhrado mārga uccārastu purīṣaṇaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 21 \[unit 329\]

bhagaṃ guhyaṃ varāṅgaṃ ca kalatraṃ janmavartma ca \|

smarāgāraṃ ratigṛhamadhodhasthādime'vyaye \|\| 21 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 22 \[unit 330\]

nā sandhiradharo'vācyadeśaśca smarakūpakaḥ \|

saṃsāramārgaśca ratikuharaṃ prakṛtiḥ striyām \|\| 22 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 23 \[unit 331\]

puṣpāpatyapathau cātha nitambaḥ syātkaṭīrakaḥ \|

liṅgaṃ tu lāṅgalaṃ śephaḥ sādhanaṃ madanāṃkuśaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 24 \[unit 332\]

smarastambhaśca kandarpamuṣalo dhvaja ityapi \|

striyāṃ nāgalatā cāṇḍakoṣastu phalakoṣakaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 25 \[unit 333\]

nābhirdvayostundakūpī tundistundīkaraḥ pumān \|

adhomarmaśakṛddvāramūruḥ sakthi ca jāghanī \|\| 25 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 26 \[unit 334\]

bhujā bāhā ca bāhau syātkulirhasto bhujādalaḥ \|

kucau vakṣoruhau proktau hastapucchaṃ tu kalpuṣam \|\| 26 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 27 \[unit 335\]

nakhaṃ punarbhavaḥ kāmāṅkuśaḥ syātkarakaṇṭakaḥ \|

kareṭuḥ karaśūkaśca pucchaṭyaṅgulimoṭanam \|\| 27 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 28 \[unit 336\]

syātkākalaṃ kaṇṭhamaṇirnasā nasyā ca nāsikā \|

gandhanālī ca nāsikyaṃ vāgdalaṃ dantavāsasi \|\| 28 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 29 \[unit 337\]

dantaḥ kharurvakrakhuro jambho hānurdvijastathā \|

rājadantāstu catvāro daśanānāṃ puraḥsthitāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 30 \[unit 338\]

godhibhālau mahāśaṅkho devadīpastu locanam \|

ambakaṃ cātha netrāmbu bāṣpo nā lotamastriyām \|\| 30 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 31 \[unit 339\]

kaṭākṣakākṣau kākustu jihvā'tha pratijihvikā \|

sudhāsravā ca karṇastu śrotaḥ klībe dhvanigrahaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 32 \[unit 340\]

syācchabdādhiṣṭhānamatha kabaraḥ keśagarbhakaḥ \|

kabarī jūṭakaścātha kauṭīraḥ syājjaṭā saṭā \|\| 32 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 33 \[unit 341\]

karāroṭoṅgulīkaḥ syāttāḍaṅkaḥ karṇadarpaṇaḥ \|

andukaḥ pādakaṭako'bhiṣekaḥ snānamityāpi \|\| 33 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 34 \[unit 342\]

paṭaccaro jīrṇavastraṃ sicayaprothaśāṭakāḥ \|

paṭavāpaḥ paṭamayaṃ dūṣyaṃ vastragṛhaṃ sthalam \|\| 34 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 35 \[unit 343\]

kharagrahaḥ kharagṛhaṃ kuvā tu sthūśāṭakaḥ \|

coloṇḍukaḥ śiroveṣṭe viraṅginyavaguṇṭhikā \|\| 35 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 36 \[unit 344\]

pāṭhī yavanikā sūryaraktasaṃjñaṃ tu kuṅkumam \|

jāguḍaṃ dīpanaṃ ghasraṃ saurabhaṃ sughṛṇaṃ ca tat \|\| 36 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 37 \[unit 345\]

syādraṅgamātṛkā lākṣā gandhakāṭhaṃ tu goṅgakam \|

kapicañcalatailākhyaḥ sihlo'tha saraladravaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 38 \[unit 346\]

dadhikṣīraghṛtāhvaḥ syāddhūnako vahnivallabhaḥ \|

śālaveṣṭaḥ sarjamaṇiḥ kastūrī gandhacelikā \|\| 38 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 39 \[unit 347\]

madāhvaścātha sañcārī dhūpo gandhapiśācikā \|

reṇusārastu karpūraścandrabhasma himāhvayaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 40 \[unit 348\]

vedhako mālayastu syācchrīkhaṇḍo rauhiṇaśca saḥ \|

vicchittistu kaṣāyaḥ syātsamālambhanamityapi \|\| 40 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 41 \[unit 349\]

lalāṭikā śaṅkhacarī viṣṭaraḥ pīṭhamastriyām \|

niṣadyā khaṭṭikā sandī cāturastu masūrakaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 42 \[unit 350\]

pratiyatnastu racanā kaṭakolaḥ patadgrahaḥ \|

pūgapīṭhaṃ ca dīpastu snehāśaḥ kajjaladhvajaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 43 \[unit 351\]

daśendhano gṛhamaṇirdoṣātilaka ityapi \|

śikhātarurdīpavṛkṣo jyotsnāvṛkṣo'tha locakaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 44 \[unit 352\]

kajjale syādiripake kandukaścodanāguḍaḥ \|

niścukkaṇaṃ dantaśāṇaṃ piṣṭāto dhūligucchakaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / manuṣyavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 353\]

iti manuṣyavargaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 354\]

vaṃśyaḥ kulyaśca vījyaḥ syādbrahmacārī vaṭuḥ samau \|

syādvaṭūkaraṇantūpanayanaṃ gṛhamedhini \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 355\]

jyeṣṭhāśramī gṛhī vānaprastho vaikhānaso'grahaḥ \|

vakrajastvanamo vipro varṇajyeṣṭhaḥ kaṭho dvijaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 356\]

maitraḥ punaruktajanmā syādatha brāhmaṇāyanaḥ \|

śuddhasantānajo vipraḥ śrautraṃ śrotriyatāṃ viduḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 357\]

syādakṣaramukhaḥ kālākṣarikaḥ śikṣitākṣaraḥ \|

kāvyasya-kartā bhavinī guṇanī śīlanaṃ smṛtam \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 358\]

kriyākaro navacchātraḥ kārpaṭikaḥ sa marmavit \|

parṣatpariṣadī goṣṭī rājasūyo nṛpādhvaraḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 359\]

kratūttamaścā'tha cāndrāyaṇaminduvrataṃ smṛtam \|

havanī homakuṇḍaṃ syānmahāvīro makhānalaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 360\]

homadhūmastu nigaṇo homabhasma tu vaiṣṭubham \|

āryakaṃ piṇḍadānādipitṛkāryamatho haviḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 361\]

hotraṃ haviṣyaṃ sānnāyyaṃ pāṭhako dharmabhāṇakaḥ \|

sabhyastu prāśnikā devadroṇī yātrā divaukasām \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 362\]

iṣṭāpūrtaṃ tadekoktyā yāgakhātādi karmma yat \|

yajñadravyantu pātrīyaṃ prādhuṇastvatithirdvayoḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 10 \[unit 363\]

kauśalī kuśalapraśnaḥ samīcī vandanā matā \|

upoṣaṇantūpavāso rogādyairlaṅghanaṃ ca tat \|\| 10 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 11 \[unit 364\]

dvayantare tryantare bhuktamāhuḥ ṣaṣṭhānnakālakam \|

rāddhaṃ siddhamatho cāruvratā māsopavāsinī \|\| 11 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 12 \[unit 365\]

syādgāyatrī tu sāvitrī brahmāraṇyaṃ tu pāṭhabhūḥ \|

pavitraṃ yajñopavītaṃ brahmasūtraṃ dvijāyanī \|\| 12 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 13 \[unit 366\]

kacchā kacchaṭikā kakṣāpaṭī kaupīnamityāpi \|

baiḍālavratikaḥ sarvābhisandhī chadmatāpasaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 14 \[unit 367\]

kamaṇḍaluścaityamukho jotiṇṭā tu mahāvratī \|

uraskaṭaḥ pañcavaṭī bālayajñopavītakam \|\| 14 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 15 \[unit 368\]

bhāle tisro bhasmarekhāstripuṇḍrakamatharṣayaḥ \|

śāpāstrāḥ satyavacaso vyāsādyāstu maharṣayaḥ \|\| 15 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 16 \[unit 369\]

paramarṣayastu bhelādyā devarṣayaḥ kaṇādayaḥ \|

brahmarṣayo vasiṣṭhādyāḥ suśrutādyāḥ śrutarṣayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 17 \[unit 370\]

ṛtarpaṇādayo rājarṣayaḥ kāṇḍarṣayastvamī \|

jaiminyādyā nāradastu kapivakro vidhātṛbhūḥ \|\| 17 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 18 \[unit 371\]

devabrahmā devalaśca durvāsāstu kusāraṇiḥ \|

pracetasastu vālmīkiḥ kavijyeṣṭhaḥ kuśīvaśaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 19 \[unit 372\]

api valmīkavālmīkau kṛtakoṭistu kāśyapaḥ \|

kṛṣṇadvaipāyano vedavyāsaḥ syātsatyabhārataḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 20 \[unit 373\]

pārāśariḥ sātyavato māṭharo bādarāyaṇaḥ \|

vasiṣṭhārundhatīnāthau viśvāmitrastu gādhijaḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 21 \[unit 374\]

gautamastu śatānandaḥ kaṇādaḥ kāśyapaḥ samau \|

dhanvantarirdivodāsaḥ kāśirājaḥ sudhodbhavaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 22 \[unit 375\]

pālakākhyo gaṇapatikareṇurucirāsutaḥ \|

viṣṇuguptastu kauṇḍiṇyaścāṇakyo dromiṇoṃ'śulaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 23 \[unit 376\]

vātsyāyano mandanāgaḥ pakṣilasvāmināvapi \|

upavarṣo halabhṛtiḥ kṛtakoṭirayācitaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 24 \[unit 377\]

pāṇinistvāhiko dākṣīputraḥ śālaṅkipāṇiṇau \|

śālotturīyo'tha vyāḍirvindhyastho nandinīsutaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 25 \[unit 378\]

medhāvī cā'tha medhājitkātyaḥ kātyāyanaśca saḥ \|

punarvasurvararucirgonardīyaḥ patañjaliḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 26 \[unit 379\]

cūrṇikṛdbhāṣyakāraśca harirbhartṛhariḥ smṛtaḥ \|

raghukāraḥ kālidāso medhārudraśca koṭijit \|\| 26 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 27 \[unit 380\]

bhāraviḥ śatrulumpaḥ syādbhavabhūtistu vismṛtaḥ \|

bhūgarbho'pya'tha yaḥ prātaḥ smaryate śubhakāmyayā \|\| 27 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 28 \[unit 381\]

sa sugṛhītanāmā syātpaiṇḍinyaṃ bhaikṣajīvikā \|

pāṣāṇḍakaulikau khañjanarataṃ yatimaithunam \|\| 28 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 29 \[unit 382\]

abhāvādaṅganātyāgasturagabrahmacaryakam \|

mukhaghaṇṭā hulahulī nāgavīṭastu ṭātkaraḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 30 \[unit 383\]

khaṭakastyayakṣaraścā'tha dārakarma karagrahaḥ \|

vādhukyaṃ ca vivāhaḥ syātkuhaliḥ pūgapuṣpikā \|\| 30 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 31 \[unit 384\]

nāndīko toraṇastambho surataṃ tvabhimānitam \|

gharṣitaṃ samprayogaṃ nā rataṃ cābrahmacāryakam \|

upasṛṣṭaṃ tribhadraṃ ca krīḍāratnaṃ mahāsukham \|\| 31 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / brahmavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 385\]

iti brahmavargaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 386\]

dvijaliṅgī nṛpaḥ kṣatraḥ kṣatriyo'tha prajeśvaraḥ \|

rājādhipau maṇḍaleśa ekajanmā bhayāpahaḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 387\]

skandhāvārastu kaṭakaḥ śibiraṃ tu balasthitiḥ \|

ādirājaḥ pṛthurvainyaḥ kākutsthastu purañjayaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 388\]

yauvanāśvastu māndhātā khaṭvāṅgastu dilīparāṭ \|

rāmo dāśarathiḥ kauśalyāyanirdaśakaṇṭhajit \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 389\]

maithilī jānakī sītā vaidehī bhūmijā ca sā \|

rāmaputrau kuśalavāvekayoktyā kuśīlavau \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 390\]

saumitrirlakṣmaṇo meghanādajiccātha rāvaṇaḥ \|

rākṣasendro daśamukho laṅkeśo dhanadānujaḥ \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 391\]

mandodarīśaḥ paulastyo meghanādastu śakrajit \|

hanūmānhanumānāñjaneyo yogacarānilī \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 392\]

hiḍimbāramaṇo rāmadūtaścaivārjunadhvajaḥ \|

aindristu bālī bāliśca sugrīvo ravinandanaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 393\]

ailaḥ purūravo baudhirurvaśīvallabhaśca saḥ \|

yayātirnāhuṣiḥ śākuntaleyo bharataḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 394\]

daumantiḥ sarvadamano'rjuno bāhusahastrabhṛt \|

haihayaḥ kārtavīryaśca puṇyaślokastu bāhukaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 10 \[unit 395\]

nalo'śvavinnaiṣadhaśca mahābhīṣmastu śāntanuḥ \|

pratīpo'sya priyā kālī dāseyī ca jhaṣodarī \|\| 10 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 11 \[unit 396\]

vicitravīryā citrāṅgadasūryojanagandhikā \|

vyāsamātā satyavatī gāṅgeyo gāndinīsutaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 12 \[unit 397\]

devavrataḥ śāntanavo gāṅgo gāṅgāyaniśca saḥ \|

bhīṣmaḥ kauṇapadantaśca svecchāmṛtyuḥ purāvasuḥ \|\| 12 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 13 \[unit 398\]

dhṛtarāṣṭrastvāmbikeyaḥ pāṇḍurmādrī pṛthāpatiḥ \|

duryodhanastu kururāṅgāndhāreyaḥ suyodhanaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 14 \[unit 399\]

dharmaputro'jamīḍhaḥ syātkarṇānujayudhiṣṭhirau \|

ajātaśatruḥ kāṅko'tha bhīmasenaḥ kaṭavraṇaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 15 \[unit 400\]

vīrareṇurnāgabalo guṇakārī vṛkodaraḥ \|

bakakīcakakirmīrajarāsandhahiḍimbajit \|\| 15 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 16 \[unit 401\]

pārthaḥ kirīṭī gāṇḍīvī guḍākeśo bṛhannalaḥ \|

arjunaḥ phālguno jiṣṇurvijayaśca dhanañjayaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 17 \[unit 402\]

savyasācī subhadreśaḥ śabdabhedī kapidhvajaḥ \|

vārtaghnaścā'pi bībhatsurdraupadī pāṇḍuśarmilā \|\| 17 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 18 \[unit 403\]

pāñcālī pārvatī kṛṣṇā cedijā nityayauvanā \|

yājñasenyatha rādheyo vasuseno'rkanandanaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 19 \[unit 404\]

ghaṭotkacāntakaḥ karṇaścampeśaḥ sūtaputrakaḥ \|

droṇastu gururācāryo'śvatthāmā tu kṛpīsutaḥ \|\| 19 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 20 \[unit 405\]

drauṇāyano'tha rājarṣirjanamejayasaṃjñakaḥ \|

pārīkṣito'pyatha jaratkāruryāyāvaro mataḥ \|\| 20 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 21 \[unit 406\]

priyā'sya manasādevī jaratkārurathaitayoḥ \|

āstīkanāmā tanayo virocanasuto baliḥ \|\| 21 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 22 \[unit 407\]

bāṇo bindhyābaliputastvāṣṭro vṛtro'pyahiśca saḥ \|

damaghoṣasutaścaidyaścedirāṭ śiśupālakaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 23 \[unit 408\]

bārhadrathirjarāsandhaḥ kaṃsaḥ syādugrasenajaḥ \|

karṇīsutau mūladevo mūlabhadraḥ kalāṅkuraḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 24 \[unit 409\]

mantrī granthiharo'mātyo dvāḥsthito vetradhārakaḥ \|

dvoḥsādhiko vartarūko garvāṭī daṇḍavāsiniḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 25 \[unit 410\]

sahapāṃśukilaḥ snehī vayaso'pya'tha vārttikaḥ \|

vārttāyanaḥ pravṛttijño mauhūrto daivalekhakaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 26 \[unit 411\]

cittoktiḥ puṣpaśakaṭī daivapraśna upaśrutiḥ \|

lekhako masipaṇyaḥ syādvolakaḥ kiriko'pi saḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 27 \[unit 412\]

masidhānī masimaṇirmelāndhurvarṇakūpikā \|

masijalaṃ tu melā syātpatrañjanaṃ masirdvayoḥ \|\| 27 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 28 \[unit 413\]

lekhanī varṇatūlī syāllekho vācikahārakaḥ \|

varṇadūtaḥ svastimukhaḥ kācanaṃ tannibandhanam \|\| 28 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 29 \[unit 414\]

lekhasthānaṃ granthakuṭī mudrā pratyayakāriṇī \|

yāturgantuśca pathiko hariḥ pathikasantatiḥ \|\| 29 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 30 \[unit 415\]

sandeśaḥ prābhṛtaṃ kauśalyutkocastūpadānakam \|

janāntikaṃ tvaprakāśo luṇḍī syānnyāyasāriṇī \|\| 30 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 31 \[unit 416\]

tāmbūlado vāggulikaḥ saṃvāhako'ṅgamardakaḥ \|

cāmaraṃ romagutsaṃ ca keśaraṃ cāvacūlakam \|\| 31 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 32 \[unit 417\]

kuṭherurmantharuḥ prototsādanaṃ vastrakuṭṭimam \|

chatraṃ puṭoṭajaṃ rājaśṛṅgaṃ kanakadaṇḍakam \|\| 32 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 33 \[unit 418\]

hastī mahāmṛgaḥ pīluḥ sindhuro dīrghamārutaḥ \|

rājīvo jalakāṅkṣaśca nirjharaḥ karaṭī kaṭī \|\| 33 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 34 \[unit 419\]

vilomajihvāntaḥsvedāpaṇḍapādalatālakāḥ \|

varāṅgaḥ puṣkarī śūrpakarṇasāmajapecilāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 35 \[unit 420\]

kariṇī vāsitā gambhīravedyaṃkuśadurdharaḥ \|

cālakavyālakau rājavāhyo vijayakuñjaraḥ \|\| 35 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 36 \[unit 421\]

īṣādanto mahādantaḥ karṇāsphāle jhalajjhalā \|

madaprayogo vyastāraḥ vikkastu kariśāvakaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 37 \[unit 422\]

ārakṣaḥ kumbhamandhiḥ syātsandāno'ṣṭhīvatoradhaḥ \|

nāsāpūrvaṃ sthūlahastaṃ dantamūle karīrikā \|\| 37 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 38 \[unit 423\]

śaṅkhaḥ kaṭo dantamadhye praveṣṭaḥ pṛṣṭhatalpane \|

āyaskāraḥ prajaṅghāgre prohaścaraṇaparvaṇi \|\| 38 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 39 \[unit 424\]

na nā gātrāvare nālī karṇārā karṇavedhanī \|

ālānaṃ śaṅkurakṣobhaḥ prārabdhirgajabandhanī \|\| 39 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 40 \[unit 425\]

prācī tu śṛṅkhalā hiñjīro'strī syādandukaśca saḥ \|

pittajvaraḥ pākalo'sya kūṭapūrvastridoṣajaḥ \|\| 40 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 41 \[unit 426\]

aśvaḥ kiṇvī harikrāntaḥ śālihotraśca mudgabhuk \|

śrīputraścāmarī heṣī rājaskandho marudrathaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 42 \[unit 427\]

vātāyanaścaikaśapho yasgtu pacāṅgapuṣpitaḥ \|

sa pañcabhadraḥ sūryāśve vātāṭaharitau samau \|\| 42 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 43 \[unit 428\]

yāyāvaro'śvamedhīyaḥ pārasīkaḥ parādanaḥ \|

āraṭṭajaśca coskastu sindhuvāro hayottamaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 44 \[unit 429\]

vātāśvatātyājāneyo veśaro'śvataraḥ kharaḥ \|

śrīvṛkṣo'sya hṛdāvarto rocamāno galodbhavaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 45 \[unit 430\]

ūrdhvasthitiḥ purūṣikaṃ vikrāntistu pulāyitam \|

pulāsannāhaprakharau kaviyaṃ kavikākocyate \|\| 45 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 46 \[unit 431\]

grīvāghaṇṭā dhardharā syādraśmiḥ pravayaṇaṃ ca tat \|

pratodo'tha khuro viḍkhaḥ puṭo luṇṭanavellane \|\| 46 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 47 \[unit 432\]

valgā dantālikā palyanaṃ paryāṇamasya tu \|

vaṅkāgrabhāgaḥ syādaśvavāro vallabhapālakaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 48 \[unit 433\]

nandīghoṣo'rjunaratho nītidhoṣastu gīṣpateḥ \|

caṅkuraṃ ghoraṇaṃ yānamātre cakraṃ tvari smṛtam \|\| 48 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 49 \[unit 434\]

ladhvī tvāṣṭrī rathī kālavalanaṃ tvaṅkavarmaṇī \|

meṭho meṇḍo hastipake jaṅghātrāṇaṃ tu maṅkhuṇam \|\| 49 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 50 \[unit 435\]

syānnāyakāgresarikau nāsīraṃ darpa ūṣmaṇi \|

śastramāyudhamudghātaḥ sthāvaraṃ tu dhanurguṇī \|\| 50 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 51 \[unit 436\]

śarāvāpo dhanuḥ strī syāttṛṇatā triṇatāpi ca \|

vyadhastu pratikāyaḥ syājjīvā syādbhāravaṃ guṇaḥ \|\| 51 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 52 \[unit 437\]

śastrābhyāsaśca khuralī bāṇaḥ syādatstrakaṇṭakaḥ \|

sthūlakṣveḍo vipāṭaśca citrapuṅkhaḥ śaraḥ saraḥ \|\| 52 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 53 \[unit 438\]

patravāho vikarṣo'tha tīraṃ vijjālasāyake \|

lohanālastu nārācaḥ prasaraḥ kāṇḍagocaraḥ \|\| 53 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 54 \[unit 439\]

ṛṣṭiḥ khaḍgaḥ taravāriḥ śastro bhadrātmajaśca naḥ \|

dhārāviṣo virāsano nyubjakhaḍgaḥ kaṭītalaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 55 \[unit 440\]

syādaṭṭanaṃ cakraphalaṃ parśuḥ paraśurityapi \|

dīrghāyudhaṃ śalaḥ kuntaḥ śalyaḥ śaṅkurviṣāṅkuraḥ \|\| 55 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 56 \[unit 441\]

śūlo'strī sīsakaṃ lagnāḥ prātargeyāḥ stutivratāḥ \|

bhogāvalī vandipāṭho vasukīṭastu yācakaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 57 \[unit 442\]

vātaketuḥ kṣitikaṇaḥ syātpāṃśurmedinīdravaḥ \|

sambhāraḥ sarvapūrṇatvaṃ patākā vyomamaḍalam \|\| 57 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 58 \[unit 443\]

kadalī kandalī cīnamakṣaṃvāṭastu mallabhūḥ \|

bhīrustu bhelo hariṇahṛdayaḥ syātpalaṅkaṭaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 59 \[unit 444\]

vidravastu śṛgālī syādāghātastu vadhasthalī \|

kadanaṃ māraṇaṃ manthaḥ sauptikaṃ rātrimāraṇam \|\| 59 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 60 \[unit 445\]

nipāto bhūmilābhaśca mṛtyurapyatha mārakaḥ \|

marako mārirutpāṭaḥ śavastu kṣitivardhanaḥ \|\| 60 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 61 \[unit 446\]

pañcāvasthaḥ kaṭaḥ sthāgaḥ śmaśānaṃ tu śatānakam \|

rudrākrīḍo dāhasaraḥ saṃskriyā satkriyā same \|\| 61 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 62 \[unit 447\]

citā kāṣṭhamaṭī caityaṃ citācūḍakamiṣyate \|

citaṃ cā'thāntaśayyāyāṃ khaṭirvāruṭha ityapi \|

upagrahastu grāhaḥ syādvandvī karamarītyapi \|\| 62 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / kṣattriyavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 448\]

iti kṣattriyavargaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 449\]

tṛṣṇā dhanapiśācī syādvārdhuṣyaṃ dhānyavardhane \|

prāmārjyamṛṇamarthānāṃ prayogaḥ syānmalāmlikā \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 450\]

ṛṇamuktirvigaṇanaṃ kṣetraṃ vāraṭamucyate \|

halirbṛhaddhale mudgo harināmā vanākhukaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 451\]

vasantaścā'tha masuro masūro vrīhikāñcanaḥ \|

sarṣapaḥ syātsariṣapaḥ kaṭusnehaśca tantubhaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 452\]

praveṭaḥ sitaśūkaḥ syādgodhūmo mlecchabhojanaḥ \|

tāmravṛntaḥ kulatthaḥ syānniḥsnehaścaṇḍikā'tasī \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 453\]

mūrkhaḥ purīṣalo māṣo rājamāṣastu varvaṭaḥ \|

phullapālaḥ śūrpavāte na strī titau cālanī \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 454\]

kaṇḍanyudūkhalaṃ prasthaḥ pāliḥ sūdaḥ pracelakaḥ \|

kuśūlo strī vrīhyagāraṃ kanduragniṣṭha ucyate \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 455\]

āliñjaro nā nandā strī mṛtsā bhāṇḍakabhṛṣṭikā \|

vārāsanaṃ vāḥsadanaṃ kvaṇano haṇḍikāsutaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 456\]

kalaśī gargarī tulyā syāccharāvastu mārttikaḥ \|

śālājiraḥ pārthivaśca mṛtkāṃsyamatha lohikā \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 457\]

kharasollaḥ kharapātraṃ śiṅghāṇaṃ kācabhājanam \|

kāṣṭhalohī tu vātardirjīrako dīpyakaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 10 \[unit 458\]

kaṭubhaṅgastu śuṇṭhī syātkāñjikaṃ tu tuṣodakam \|

gṛhāmlaṃ siddhasalilaṃ rakṣoghnaṃ dhātunāśanam \|\| 10 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 11 \[unit 459\]

gṛhiṇī madhurā hiṅgu śūlahṛtsūpadhūpanam \|

varṣiṇī hemarāgiṇyo haridrā pītavālukā \|\| 11 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 12 \[unit 460\]

gaṇḍolaṃ syādguḍaḥ svāduḥ pipyaṭā guḍaśarkarā \|

mahāśvetā tu madhujā prayastaṃ syātsusaṃskṛtam \|\| 12 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 13 \[unit 461\]

cipiṭo dhānyacamasaścitrāpūpaścaruvraṇaḥ \|

ālimpanā tarpaṇā dīpanaṃ maṇḍodakaṃ ca tat \|\| 13 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 14 \[unit 462\]

vaṭakaḥ piṣṭapūraḥ syādvratopāyanavāyane \|

praheṇakaṃ cā'tha pālī pūpālī carpaṭī ca sā \|\| 14 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 15 \[unit 463\]

saktuśvūrṇaka āpūpyo bhaktaṃ kuruḥ prasādanaḥ \|

maṇḍo jyeṣṭhāmbu khadikā lājāḥ pārī tu dohanī \|\| 15 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 16 \[unit 464\]

nipānaṃ strī gavī tumbā nilimpā rohiṇī ca sā \|

gavyaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ puṃsavanaṃ syāddohāpanayaḥ pumān \|\| 16 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 17 \[unit 465\]

avadāhaḥ payaḥ kṣīraṃ prāggāḍhaṃ śrīdhanaṃ dadhi \|

kadvaraṃ tu dadhisneho jagdhirbhojanabhakṣaṇe \|\| 17 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 18 \[unit 466\]

dehayātrā viṣvaṇanaṃ bhuktaṃ pratyavasānavat \|

kalyavartaḥ prātarāśaḥ prātarbhojanamityapi \|\| 18 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 19 \[unit 467\]

anaḍvāñśāṅkaro gandhamaithunaḥ śakkarirvṛṣaḥ \|

kakudmānpuṅgavaścā'tha kaṇṭhālā jālagoṇikā \|\| 19 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 20 \[unit 468\]

dogdhā śakṛtkarirvatsasteṣāmāṭīkanaṃ kramaḥ \|

gavādikaṃ jīvadhanaṃ paliknī bālagarbhiṇī \|\| 20 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 21 \[unit 469\]

karakaṃ gomayacchatre gogranthiḥ śuṣkagomayam \|

karīṣaśchagaṇo hambhā rebhaṇaṃ ca gavāṃ dhvanau \|\| 21 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 22 \[unit 470\]

naicikaṃ goḥ śirodeśo vandanīyā tu rocanā \|

manthānamanthatakrāṭāḥ syāduṣṭraḥ kaṇṭakāśanaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 23 \[unit 471\]

lambauṣṭhādhvagavāsantakulanāśamarudvipāḥ \|

bholirbahukaro vakragrīvo meṣastu romaśaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 24 \[unit 472\]

saṃsphālahuḍubheḍāśca meṣī syājjālakinyaviḥ \|

varkarastu lambakarṇaśchāgo bastaḥ śivāpriyaḥ \|\| 24 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 25 \[unit 473\]

alpāyurladhukāyaśca menādaḥ parṇabhojanaḥ \|

parikramasaho bukko devānāṃpriya ityapi \|\| 25 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 26 \[unit 474\]

chāgī sañjā culampā syācchaṅkukarṇastu gardabhaḥ \|

smarasmaryaściramehī grāmyāśvo reṇurūṣitaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 27 \[unit 475\]

paśuścariḥ śuddhajaṅgho ratnaṃ cāruśilā maṇiḥ \|

svādhyāyī pattanavaṇigdhoṭābukkośvavikrayī \|\| 27 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 28 \[unit 476\]

sarvamūlyaṃ dyūtabījaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca kapardakaḥ \|

vaṭakaḥ paṇāsthi syādbhāsuraḥ sphāṭikopalaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 29 \[unit 477\]

śālipiṣṭaṃ dhautaśilaṃ galvarkaśca susāravat \|

aśmasārastvindranīlo vaidūryaṃ bālavāyajam \|\| 29 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 30 \[unit 478\]

bālasūryamatho raktakandalo vidrumaśca saḥ \|

latāmaṇiḥ pravālaḥ syādrājabhaṭṭo virāṭajaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 31 \[unit 479\]

kuruvindaḥ padmarāgo dadhīcyasthi tu hīrakam \|

svarṇaṃ lohavaraṃ cā'gnibījaṃ cāmpeyamityapi \|\| 31 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 32 \[unit 480\]

rūpye śvetaṃ raṅgabījamindukānte maṇīcakam \|

tāmrakaṃ tu lohitāyaḥ sūryāhvaṃ munipittalam \|\| 32 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 33 \[unit 481\]

kaṃse tu kaṃsaṃ kaṃsāsthi tāmrārdhamatha mauktikam \|

rasopalaṃ śuktibījaṃ sindūraṃ raktavālukam \|\| 33 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 34 \[unit 482\]

pāradaḥ siddhadhātuḥ syāddharabījaṃ ca sūtakam \|

raṅgaṃ surebhaṃ mṛdvaṅgaṃ kusumbhaṃ grāmyakuṅkumam \|\| 34 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 35 \[unit 483\]

haritāle tu karbūraṃ godanto naṭasaṃjñakaḥ \|

dāradaṃ tu hiḍgulu syāddhiṅgulaṃ raktarapāradam \|\| 35 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 36 \[unit 484\]

rasagandho gandharaso gāndhāraṃ masivardhanam \|

kṣārastu syādyavakṣāro yavaśūkaśca recakaḥ \|

śilājatu śilāvyādhistṛphalā triphalā'pi ca \|\| 36 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 485\]

iti vaiśyavargaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 1 \[unit 486\]

śudraḥ syātpādajo dāso grāmakūṭo mahattaraḥ \|

mālikaḥ puṣpalāvaḥ syākumbhakārastu mṛtkaraḥ \|\| 1 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 2 \[unit 487\]

daṇḍabhṛtpalagaṇḍastu sudhājīvī ca lepakaḥ \|

kāyasthe kūṭakṛtpañjīkarau citrakare kṛṇuḥ \|\| 2 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 3 \[unit 488\]

carmaruḥ kuraṭaḥ pādukṛtkalādastu gauñjikaḥ \|

nāḍindhamaḥ svarṇakāro vātsīputrastu nāpitaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 4 \[unit 489\]

candrilo nakhakuṭṭaśca rajakaḥ karmakīlakaḥ \|

kanyāpālaḥ pālavaṇigdevalatvamadvijaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 5 \[unit 490\]

ajājīvastu jāvālo niṣkaścaṇḍāla ucyate \|

kukkure dīrghasurato vāntādarasanālihau \|\| 5 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 6 \[unit 491\]

ratavraṇau grāmamṛgastathendramahakānukaḥ \|

kapilo vakrapucchaśca śayālurarapatrapaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 7 \[unit 492\]

śunyāḥ śunyaṃ śunīraṃ syāccauraḥ śaṅkitavarṇakaḥ \|

syātkumbhilaśca pracurapurūṣo'tha kujambhilaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 8 \[unit 493\]

suruṅgo'hivadhaścore vandīkāratu mācalaḥ \|

prasahyacoraścillābhaḥ strīcoro ratahiṇḍakaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 9 \[unit 494\]

kāvyacoraścandrareṇuḥ kuḍyacchedyaṃ tu khānikam \|

sandhiḥ suraṅkā tadbhedāḥ śrīvatsagomukhādayaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 10 \[unit 495\]

kapālalāsikā tarkustarkuśāṇastu jhāmakaḥ \|

vartanī tarkupīṭhī syātpiñjanaṃ tūlakārmukam \|\| 10 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 11 \[unit 496\]

tūlaḥ picuḥ picutūlaṃ piñjikā tulanālikā \|

triṣu dvārāpaṇaṃ sūtraparṇaṃ yattena veṣṭyate \|\| 11 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 12 \[unit 497\]

nirveṭanaṃ nāḍicīramekāṣṭī tulaśarkarā \|

tantrakāṣṭhaṃ turī strī syādgrīṣmamahāsendratūlake \|\| 12 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 13 \[unit 498\]

pannadhrī pādukā prāṇihitā padarathītyapi \|

kāvārī jaṅgamukuṭī bhramatkuṭyatha kharparaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 14 \[unit 499\]

vāritrā mūrdhakholañca saiva patrapiśācikā \|

vāsī tu takṣaṇī na strī krakacaḥ patradārakaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|

## dvitīyaḥ kāṇḍaḥ / vaiśyavargaḥ / 15 \[unit 500\]

madyaṃ tvariṣṭaṃ maireyaṃ kattoyaṃ kāpiśāyanam \|
